| `Roth.genWronskian` | `Roth.coeffNone` | no | A: Defines the determinant whose rows apply prescribed multivariate Hasse derivatives to a finite polynomial family. Mechanism: Forms a square Matrix.of of hasseDeriv entries and takes its determinant. B: Extract the polynomial coefficient of a fixed power of the distinguished variable. Mechanism: Compose the standard optionEquivLeft algebra equivalence with Polynomial.coeff. Generalized Wronskian nonvanishing selects derivative rows for an independent family. Separation first constructs independent factor families and then uses that selection together with a univariate Wronskian and determinant multiplication. Its factor integrality requires coefficient primitivity/descent beyond characteristic-zero linear algebra.
